[EVENT: TUR30653]
Date: 2023-08-19
Country: Turkey | Location: Van
Type: Strategic developments > Change to group/activity | Disorder: Strategic developments
Actor1: Government of Turkey (2016-) [State forces]
Fatalities: 0
Sources: Kurdistan24; Rudaw [Regional]

DESCRIPTION:
Security measures: On 19 August 2023, Van Governor's office issued a ban on all forms of marches, protests, press conferences, sit-ins and other political gatherings in the province for 2 days in order to maintain public order and security. Similar measures have been imposed in the province (and several other southeastern provinces) for some years to maintain public order, presumably against political events that can be organized by Kurdish movements.

[EVENT: TUR30661]
